Optimization algorithm generic:

- Define score(p) = max(np.convolve(p,p)) / sum(p)^2? Since sum fixed1, max_coeff. But if scaling not fixed, we normalize after each step.

  maxc = c.max()
  Weights w: Need emphasize active constraints. Let w = np.exp(β*(c - maxc)) or (c / maxc)^q clipped. Normalize sum w.
  Compute derivative r_i = 2 * Σ_k w_k p[(k-i) mod L]. For small n, can compute by direct loops over k where w_k>tol? c length 2n-1 ≤1023; n≤512; O(n^2) ok. Direct simpler: initialize r zeros length n. For each k in range(2n-1) if w_k>0: add w_k * np.roll? Specifically r_i += w_k * p[k-i] for valid. We can for each k add to r the reversed segment of p length min(n,k+?)... Use FFT for elegance but direct okay for n≤256 and many k: 2n^2 ~250k per iter; 10000 iters=2.5e9 too high. Let's design a custom optimizer for direct p with n=64 using subgradient and restarts.
Pseudo:
import numpy as np, time, math, random

def exact_score(seq): return evaluate_sequence(seq) maybe for final.
def conv_max(p): c=np.convolve(p,p); return c.max()
For n small, np.convolve fine.

def smooth_max_value(p, beta):
 c=np.convolve(p,p); z=c/(c.max()+eps); return np.exp(beta*z) weights.

def update(p, w, eta):
 L=next_power2(2*n)
 Fp=np.fft.fft(p,L)
 Fw=np.fft.fft(w,L)
 r=np.fft.ifft(Fw*np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]*2
 # normalize gradient
 r = (r - r.mean()) / (r.max()+1e-12)
 logp = np.log(p+1e-300) - eta*r
 pnew = np.exp(logp - logp.max())
 pnew/=pnew.sum()
 return pnew, conv_max(pnew)

Line search: for eta in steps. Also maybe if all eta worse, try eta small and random noise.

Objective function:
def make(a_flat):
   f = np.empty(n)
   x = grid
   for m (1..M): f += a[(2m-1)]*np.cos(π*m*x)+a[2m]*np.sin(π*m*x) +1
   f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
   p=f/f.sum()
   c=np.convolve(p,p)
   return 2*n*c.max()

Let's outline final code:
import numpy as np, time, math
try import scipy.optimize as sco (allowed)

Helpers:
def next_pow2(n): return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()

def make_seq(coeff, n, basis):
   f = 1.0 + basis @ coeff
   f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
   s=f.sum()
   if s <=1e-12: return None
   p=f/s
   return p

def score_p(p):
   c=np.convolve(p,p)
   return 2*len(p)*c.max()  # sum normalized 1

```
def next_power2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

Fast score:
```
def fast_conv_max(p, L):
    n=p.size
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
    # clip tiny negative?
    cm=c.max()
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.inf
    return float(2*n*cm/(s*s))
```
We normalize p before, s≈1.

Mode objective:
```
def make_mode_p(vals, basis, n, clip_min=0.0):
    f = 1.0 + basis.dot(vals)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s=f.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    p=f/s
    return p
```
If using linear + modes, basis shape n x D.
Bounds list: [(-3.0,3.0)]*D.
Maybe D large, objective can have flat scaling? Since constant fixed1, no all-zero scale issue.

DE optimization function:
```
def optimize_modes(n, M, seed, time_left):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    cols=[]
    cols.append(2*x-1) # linear
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       cols.append(np.cos(math.pi*m*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(math.pi*m*x))
    basis=np.column_stack(cols)
    L=next_power2(2*n-1)
    def obj(vals):
       p=make_mode_p(vals,basis,n)
       if p is None: return 1e9
       return fast_conv_max(p,L)
    zero=np.zeros(basis.shape[1])
    t0=time.monotonic(); f0=obj(zero); t_eval=max(1e-6,time.monotonic()-t0)
    best_x=zero; best_f=f0
    if HAVE_SCIPY:
       bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*basis.shape[1]
       # run several DE
       while time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
           maxfev = int(min(8000, max(1000, ((deadline-time.monotonic()-20)*0.9)/t_eval/ (runs_remaining?) )))
```
Simpler: set number runs based on eval time:

```
   res=opt.differential_evolution(... maxfev=maxfev, popsize=20, seed=seed+run, polish=True)
```

```
def refine_p(p, iters=200, deadline=None):
   n=p.size; L=next_power2(2*n-1)
   best=p.copy(); best_sc=fast_conv_max(best,L)
   curr=p.copy(); curr_sc=fast_conv_max(curr,L)
   K=0
   for it in range(iters):
      if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline-30: break
      c=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(curr,L)**2? Can't square rfft because complex? Use full fft or rfft real ifft. 
      # rfft returns complex for k=0..L/2; square corresponds to convolution? For real, if P=rfft(x), ifft(P*P) gives linear conv? Since rfft stores half, multiplication yields conjugate symmetry if using irfft of P*P? Yes, because P*P is DFT of convolution, and irfft assumes hermitian; product of real FFT is hermitian. Good.
      P=np.fft.rfft(curr,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
      cm=c.max(); if not finite: break
      # weights: normalize c
      # use smooth max weights
      beta=20
      w=np.exp(beta*(c/cm if cm>0 else 0))
      w/=w.sum()
      # derivative r
      Fp=np.fft.rfft(curr,L)
      Fw=np.fft.fft(w,L)  # complex full because ifft needs full? Could use rfft for w real too: Fw_full? ifft expects full length; use np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(w,L)*np.conj(np.fft.fft(curr,L))).
      r=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(w,L)*np.conj(np.fft.fft(curr,L))).real[:n]*2
      r = r - r.mean()
      norm=np.max(np.abs(r))
      if norm <1e-12: break
      r /= norm
      improved=False
      for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0):
          p2=curr*np.exp(-eta*r)
          p2=np.clip(p2,1e-300,None)
          p2/=p2.sum()
          sc2=fast_conv_max(p2,L)
          if sc2 < curr_sc - 1e-10:
             curr=p2; curr_sc=sc2; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         # try random perturb
         for _ in range(3):

      if sc2 < best_sc: best=p2; best_sc=sc2
      if not improved: K+=1; if K>50: break
      else K=0
   return best,best_sc
```

```
def evaluate_p(p):
   if p is None or p.size==0: return np.inf
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return np.inf
   # ensure nonnegative finite

   return evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
```

Let's draft code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    import scipy.optimize as sopo
    HAVE_SCIPY=True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY=False

def _next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << max(0, (int(m)-1).bit_length())

def _build_basis(n,M):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    cols=[2*x-1]
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       th=math.pi*m*x
       cols.append(np.cos(th))
       cols.append(np.sin(th))
    return np.column_stack(cols)

def _make_p(vals,basis):
   f=1.0 + basis @ vals
   f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
   s=f.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
   p=f/s
   return p

def _fast_score(p,L):
   n=p.size
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)
   if c.size >= 2*n-1: c=c[:2*n-1]
   cm=c.max()
   ss=p.sum()
   if ss<=1e-12: return np.inf
   return float(2*n*cm/(ss*ss))
```
Actually `P*P`: For rfft, multiplying complex by itself not conjugate; that's correct for linear convolution? DFT of convolution x*x is P*P (not conj). Yes, because both same sequence not reversed? Wait convolution x*y has DFT X(ω)Y(ω). For x=y, P*P. For same real, product is fine. For autocorrelation (reverse) would be P*conj(P). Good.

But careful: `np.fft.rfft` returns positive freqs including Nyquist. Product P*P is conjugate symmetric? If P[k] has conjugate symmetry P[L-k]=conj(P[k]), P[k]^2 has conjugate symmetry? (conj)^2=conj(P^2), yes. So irfft okay.

Mode objective should be robust to array library:
```
def _mode_objective(vals,basis,L):
   vals=np.asarray(vals,float)
   p=_make_p(vals,basis)
   if p is None: return 1e9
   return _fast_score(p,L)
```

Search function:
```
def _search_modes(n,M,seed,deadline,min_time=10):
   basis=_build_basis(n,M); L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   D=basis.shape[1]
   def obj(v): return _mode_objective(v,basis,L)
   zero=np.zeros(D)
   best_x=zero; best_f=obj(zero)
   if not HAVE_SCIPY:
       # simple random
       rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
       t=time.monotonic()
       for i in range(20000):
          if time>deadline: break
          v=rng.uniform(-2,2,D)
          f=obj(v)
          if f<best_f: best_x=v; best_f=f
       return best_x,best_f,basis,L
   bounds=[(-2.5,2.5)]*D
   # timing one eval
   t0=time.monotonic(); f0=obj(zero); t_eval=max(1e-6,time.monotonic()-t0)
   # Estimate runs
   time_left=deadline-time.monotonic()
   runs = max(1, int(min(6, time_left/(t_eval*4000))))
   for run in range(runs):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: break
       maxfev = int(min(12000, max(2000, time_left/(t_eval*max(1,runs-run)))))
       try:
          res=sopo.differential_evolution(obj,bounds,seed=seed+run,maxfev=maxfev,popsize=20,tol=1e-6,polish=False, init='latinhypercube')
       except TypeError:
          res=sopo.differential_evolution(obj,bounds,seed=seed+run,maxfev=maxfev,popsize=20,tol=1e-6,polish=False)
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
          if res.fun < best_f:
             best_x=res.x.copy(); best_f=float(res.fun)
          # polish from res.x
          if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15:
             try:
                res2=sopo.minimize(obj,best_x,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':2000,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-7})
                if np.isfinite(res2.fun) and res2.fun<best_f:
                   best_x=res2.x; best_f=float(res2.fun)
             except Exception: pass
       time_left=deadline-time.monotonic()
   return best_x,best_f,basis,L
```

```
def consider(p):
   if p is None: return
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   if p.size==0: return
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   p=np.minimum(p,1000.0)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or p.sum()<0.01: return
   # maybe normalize? Do not change score? Evaluator scale invariant; if sum small? We can normalize to sum1 to avoid sum<0.01 and cap. It won't change score if positive. 
   s=p.sum()
   if s > 1e-12: p=p/s
   sc=evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best['score']:
       best['p']=p.copy(); best['score']=float(sc)
```

```
    L_f = _next_pow2(2*n_final-1); basis_f=_build_basis(n_final,M)
    p_f = _make_p(best_x,basis_f)
    if p_f is not None: consider(p_f)
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline-120:
       def objF(v): pf=_make_p(v,basis_f); return _fast_score(pf,L_f) if pf is not None else 1e9
       res=sopo.minimize(objF,best_x,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':3000,...})
       if np.isfinite(res.fun):
          pf=_make_p(res.x,basis_f); consider(pf)
```

```
   cur=p.copy(); cur_sc=tfast(cur); best_p=p; best_sc=cur_sc

      p2=cur.copy()

      p2=np.maximum(p2,0)
      ss=p2.sum()

      elif sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
          cur=p2
   # But best by fast may not be exact best; consider best_p and cur
   consider(best_p); consider(cur)
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _sopo
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << max(0, (m - 1).bit_length())


def _fast_score_from_p(p: np.ndarray, L: int) -> float:
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(cm) or cm < 0.0:
        cm = max(0.0, cm)
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


def _make_p_from_mode_coeffs(vals, basis) -> np.ndarray:
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    f = 1.0 + basis.dot(vals)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    p = f / s
    return p


def _build_mode_basis(n: int, M: int) -> np.ndarray:
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    cols = [2.0 * x - 1.0]
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        th = math.pi * float(m) * x
        cols.append(np.cos(th))
        cols.append(np.sin(th))
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def _mode_search(n: int, M: int, seed: int, deadline: float,
                 max_runs: int, baseline_bounds: float):
    basis = _build_mode_basis(n, M)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    D = basis.shape[1]

    def obj(vals):
        p = _make_p_from_mode_coeffs(vals, basis)
        if p is None:
            return 1e9
        return _fast_score_from_p(p, L)

    zero = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    best_x = zero.copy()
    best_f = float(obj(zero))

    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        for _ in range(max(500, int(min(20000, max(0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 10) * 500))))):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            v = rng.uniform(-baseline_bounds, baseline_bounds, D)
            f = float(obj(v))
            if math.isfinite(f) and f < best_f:
                best_f = f
                best_x = v.astype(np.float64)
        return best_x, best_f, basis, L

    # Estimate per-evaluation cost and choose a wall-clock-bounded maxfev.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    obj(zero)
    t_eval = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)

    bounds = [(-baseline_bounds, baseline_bounds)] * D
    runs_done = 0
    for run in range(max_runs):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        remaining = max(10.0, deadline - now)
        wall = min(160.0, max(12.0, 0.28 * remaining))
        maxfev = int(max(1500, min(8000, wall / max(t_eval, 1e-6))))
        try:
            try:
                res = _sopo.differential_evolution(
                    obj, bounds, seed=seed + run, maxfev=maxfev,
                    popsize=20, tol=1e-6, polish=False,
                    init="latinhypercube"
                )
            except TypeError:
                res = _sopo.differential_evolution(
                    obj, bounds, seed=seed + run, maxfev=maxfev,
                    popsize=20, tol=1e-6, polish=False
                )
            if res.x is not None and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                if res.fun < best_f:
                    best_f = float(res.fun)
                    best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()

                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
                    try:
                        res2 = _sopo.minimize(
                            obj, best_x, method="Nelder-Mead",
                            options={"maxiter": 2000, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-7}
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(res2.fun) and res2.fun < best_f:
                            best_f = float(res2.fun)
                            best_x = np.asarray(res2.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    except Exception:
                        pass
        except Exception:
            pass
        runs_done += 1

    return best_x, best_f, basis, L


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best = {"p": None, "score": float("inf")}

    def consider_array(p):
        if p is None:
            return
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size == 0:
            return
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        p = np.minimum(p, 1000.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            return
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return
        # Rescale; the evaluator's objective is homogeneous in the positive
        # scaling domain, so this does not change the legitimate score.
        p = p / s
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            return
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best["score"]:
            best["z"] = None  # just a placeholder for JSON-serializable bookkeeping
            best["score"] = sc
            best["p"] = p.copy()

    # Safe fallbacks.
    consider_array(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))

    # Use the supplied parent construction directly if present.
    try:
        parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent_obj is not None:
            parent = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parent.size and np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
                consider_array(parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    search_ns = (256, 512)
    mode_schedule = [
        (3, 2, 2.0),
        (4, 3, 2.2),
        (5, 2, 2.4),
    ]

    for n_search, M, bound_scale in mode_schedule:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        try:
            best_x, best_f, basis, L = _mode_search(
                n_search, M, seed, deadline, 2, bound_scale
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        p_search = _make_p_from_mode_coeffs(best_x, basis)
        if p_search is not None:
            consider_array(p_search)

        # Evaluate a slightly finer reconstruction of the same mode shape.
        for n_final in search_ns:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                break
            try:
                basis_f = _build_mode_basis(n_final, M)
                p_final = _make_p_from_mode_coeffs(best_x, basis_f)
                if p_final is not None:
                    consider_array(p_final)

                # Optional short local polish in continuous mode space.
                if _HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
                    L_f = _next_pow2(2 * n_final - 1)

                    def obj_final(vals, basis_f=basis_f, L_f=L_f):
                        pf = _make_p_from_mode_coeffs(vals, basis_f)
                        if pf is None:
                            return 1e9
                        return _fast_score_from_p(pf, L_f)

                    try:
                        res = _sopo.minimize(
                            obj_final, best_x, method="Nelder-Mead",
                            options={"maxiter": 2500, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-7}
                        )
                        if np.isfinite(res.fun):
                            pf_poly = _make_p_from_mode_coeffs(res.x, basis_f)
                            if pf_poly is not None:
                                consider_array(pf_poly)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Light local polish on the best exact-so-far vector, using fast surrogate
    # scoring only.  The final accepted candidate is always re-evaluated exactly.
    if best["p"] is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        try:
            cur = np.asarray(best["p"], dtype=np.float64).copy()
            n = cur.size
            L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
            cur_sc = _fast_score_from_p(cur, L)
            stop_at = deadline - 35.0
            improvement_streak = 0
            for _ in range(20000):
                if time.monotonic() > stop_at:
                    break
                w = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, n // 12)))
                s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
                cand = cur.copy()
                cand[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=w))
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                ss = float(cand.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand = cand / ss
                sc = _fast_score_from_p(cand, L)
                if math.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < best["score"] / (2.0 * n) * (2.0 * n and 1.0) or sc < cur_sc:
                        cur = cand
                        cur_sc = sc
                        improvement_streak = 0
                    else:
                        improvement_streak += 1
                        if improvement_streak > 120:
                            break
            consider_array(cur)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Last-ditch fallback: revisit the parent if available, because the exact
    # evaluator is authoritative.
    try:
        parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if parent_obj is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            parent = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parent.size and np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
                consider_array(parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best["p"] is None:
        p = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        p = p / p.sum()
        best["p"] = p

    out = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best["p"]).ravel()]
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0 or len(out) == 0:
        # Absolute emergency fallback; always finite and legal.
        out = [0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25]
    return out
```
